| [hwIAMFExportOptions GetEncoder](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwiamfexportoptions_getencoder.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `hwIAMFExportOptions_handle GetEncoder` | This command retrieves the type of encoding used when exporting AMF video files: "jpeg" AMF video frames are compressed as JPEGs. This encoding uses the quality setting returned by the hwIAMFExportOptions GetQuality function. This type of encoding can produce a smaller video file than ZLIB encoding, but it is not lossless. "zlib" AMF video frames are compressed by ZLIB. This type of encoding is lossless, but tends to produce a larger video file than JPEG encoding.Note: This parameter is used when exporting AMF files from any client during video capture. | Not stated |
| [hwIAMFExportOptions GetPalette](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwiamfexportoptions_getpalette.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `hwIAMFExportOptions_handle GetPalette` | This command retrieves the palette used when exporting AMF files: "fullcolor" A full color palette is used when exporting AVI files. "grayscale" A grayscale palette is used when exporting AVI files. | Not stated |
| [hwIAMFExportOptions GetQuality](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwiamfexportoptions_getquality.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `hwIAMFExportOptions_handle GetQuality` | This command retrieves the quality used when exporting AMF files using JPEG encoding. This is a floating point value between 0 and 100 (exclusive). A large quality means better image fidelity but larger image size.Note: This parameter is used when exporting AMF files from any client during video capture. | Not stated |
| [hwIAMFExportOptions GetSize](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwiamfexportoptions_getsize.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `hwIAMFExportOptions_handle GetSize` | This command retrieves the relative size (percent) used when exporting AMF files. This is an integer value, with possible values including 100, 75, 50, or 33.Note: This parameter is used when exporting AMF files from either the video or animation clients during video capture. | Not stated |

| [poI3DViewCtrl GetActiveView](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getactiveview.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle GetActiveView` | In order to edit a stored view, it must be marked as active. This command retrieves the name of the current active view. If no stored view is marked active, “Current View” is returned. Current View is always active. Whatever is currently displayed in the window will always be the same as the active view. | Not stated |
| [poI3DViewCtrl GetAlignedImagePlane](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getalignedimageplane.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle GetAlignedImagePlane name` | Returns an ID of image planes. It represents the image plane associated with the view for recalculating view properties given the cropping situation. If the returned ID is 0, it indicates that this view is not associated with any image plane. | **Inputs:** name If specified, the offset of the view with that name will be returned. Otherwise, the current view’s offset will be returned. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl GetCropping](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getcropping.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle GetCropping name` | Returns a string consisting of top, bottom, left, and right values. These values represent numbers of pixels pre-cropped from each corresponding side of the image plane. Captured videos/images are often processed before using them for video overlaying, one typical process is cropping the borders of a video/image. | **Inputs:** name If specified, the offset of the view with that name will be returned. Otherwise, the current view’s offset will be returned. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl GetCurrentViewString](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getcurrentviewstring.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle GetCurrentViewString` | This command retrieves the string name of the current view. This is used within HyperView to update the current view. | Not stated |
| [poI3DViewCtrl GetFocalLength](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getfocallength.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle GetFocalLength name` | The focal length value simulates the focal length of a camera lens. Adjusting this value is similar to zooming. This command retrieves a floating point value. | **Inputs:** name If specified, the focal length of the view with that name will be returned. Otherwise, the current view’s focal length will be returned. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl GetImagePlaneHeight](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getimageplaneheight.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle GetImagePlaneHeight name` | Returns the image plane height in term of number of pixels. This image plane is the one associated with the view. Along with width, it is used to recalculate view properties given the cropping situation. | **Inputs:** name If specified, the offset of the view with that name will be returned. Otherwise, the current view’s offset will be returned. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl GetImagePlaneWidth](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getimageplanewidth.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle GetImagePlaneWidth name` | Returns the image plane width in term of number of pixels. This image plane is the one associated with the view. Along with height, it is used to recalculate view properties given the cropping situation. | **Inputs:** name If specified, the offset of the view with that name will be returned. Otherwise, the current view’s offset will be returned. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl GetInputPrincipalPointOffset](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getinputprincipalpointoffset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle GetInputPrincipalPointOffset name` | Returns a string consisting of an x and y offset. These values represent a shift of the view horizontally and vertically in the window. The values are typically the same as the manufacturer specifications of a camera. | **Inputs:** name If specified, the offset of the view with that name will be returned. Otherwise, the current view’s offset will be returned. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl GetInputSensorHeight](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getinputsensorheight.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle GetInputSensorHeight name` | Returns a float number representing the input sensor height in mm. This value is typically the same as the manufacturer specifications of a camera lens. | **Inputs:** name If specified, the height of the view with that name will be returned. Otherwise, the current view’s height will be returned. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl GetLookAt](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getlookat.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle GetLookAt name` | This command retrieves nine float numbers. The first three numbers represent the position of the camera (eye). The next three numbers represent the position that the camera is looking at (center). The last three numbers represent the orientation of the camera (up). | **Inputs:** name If specified, the lookat of the view with that name will be returned. Otherwise, the current view’s lookat will be returned. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl GetMultiPointAligned](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getmultipointaligned.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle GetMultiPointAligned name` | Returns true if the view is created from an image plane alignment operation, and false otherwise. This status is useful to determine whether the image plane ID of the view can be changed. If the return value is true, the view is associated with this image plane in life time. | **Inputs:** name If specified, the offset of the view with that name will be returned. Otherwise, the current view’s offset will be returned. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl GetOrientation](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getorientation.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `viewControl_handle GetOrientation` | Returns the orientation of the model within the view. Six floats are returned describing the translation of the model as Tx, Ty, and Tz, and three Euler angles representing the rotation about the Z, X, and Z axes, respectively. This command is valid for both the orthographic projection and perspective projection modes. | Not stated |
| [poI3DViewCtrl GetOrtho](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getortho.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `viewControl_handle GetOrtho` | Returns the parameters which define the view when in the orthogonal projection mode. The returned values represent the min and max clipping planes for X, Y, and Z respectively. | Not stated |
| [poI3DViewCtrl GetPerspectiveAngle](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getperspectiveangle.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle GetPerspectiveAngle name` | This command retrieves a float number representing the perspective angle of a view. | **Inputs:** name If specified, the perspective angle of the view with that name will be returned. Otherwise, the current view’s perspective angle will be returned. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl GetPerspectiveMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getperspectivemode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle GetPerspectiveMode` | This command turns the perspective mode on or off. | Not stated |
| [poI3DViewCtrl GetPrincipalPointOffset](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getprincipalpointoffset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle GetPrincipalPointOffset name` | This command retrieves a string consisting of an x and y offset. These values represent a shift of the view horizontally and vertically in the window. | **Inputs:** name If specified, the offset of the view with that name will be returned. Otherwise, the current view’s offset will be returned. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl GetProjectionType](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getprojectiontype.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle GetProjectionType name` | This command retrieves either Orthographic, Perspective, or Lens. Orthographic and Perspective are the traditional HyperView modes. The Lens type simulates a camera lens. | **Inputs:** name If specified, the type of the view with that name will be returned. Otherwise, the current view’s type will be returned. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl GetSensorHeight](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getsensorheight.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle GetSensorHeight name` | This command retrieves a float number representing the sensor height in mm. This value simulates the sensor height of a camera lens. | **Inputs:** name If specified, the height of the view with that name will be returned. Otherwise, the current view’s height will be returned. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl GetUpOrientationMethod](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getuporientationmethod.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `viewControl_handle GetUpOrientationMethod name` | Returns the orientation method used to define the camera top position. | **Inputs:** name String specifying the name of the view to be queried. The current view will be queried if a name is not specified. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl GetUpOrientationVector](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getuporientationvector.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `viewControl_handle GetUpOrientationVector name` | Returns the orientation vector of the camera top position. | **Inputs:** name String specifying the name of the view to be queried. The current view will be queried if a name is not specified. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl GetViewList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getviewlist.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle GetViewList view_list` | This command retrieves a list of all memorized views in the active window. | **Inputs:** view_list The list that contains all of the view names for the active window. |

| [poI3DViewCtrl GetWithMasking](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getwithmasking.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle GetWithMasking name` | Returns the state of the "With Masking" flag for the view with the given name. | [poI3DViewCtrl GetZOffset](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_getzoffset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle GetZOffset name` | This command retrieves the z offset of a stored view. | **Inputs:** name If specified, the z offset of the view with that name will be returned. SYSID System ID. LABEL Cross Section Label. REVIEW Mode to review Cross Section. The above properties are used to set the CrossSection Property in the SetCrossSectionProperty command and the GetCrossSectionProperty command. |

| [poIImagePlane GetAlignmentData](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_getalignmentdata.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_handle GetAlignmentData idx` | This command returns the list of points stored in the 2D image plane, which have been used (or would be used) to achieve a multipoint alignment between a model and the image plane. The idx parameter can be used to query the specific point with index equals idx from the list. | **Inputs:** idx (Optional) Point index in the list of alignment points. |
| [poIImagePlane GetAlignmentMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_getalignmentmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_handle GetAlignmentMode` | An image plane in 3D mode can be aligned with a model to check correlation between a video and the simulation. The alignment can be performed using three points on the video and on the model, or using two points and a principal plane. This command returns the mode that was used to align the image plane. The mode determines what data will get returned when calling the GetAlignmentData command. This command returns “2POINT” for 2 point alignment, and “3POINT” for 3 point alignment. | Not stated |
| [poIImagePlane GetAlignmentPointList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_getalignmentpointlist.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_handle GetAlignmentPointList pt_idx` | This command queries information about an alignment point. | **Inputs:** pt_idx The index of the alignment point to be queried. Index starts at 0. |
| [poIImagePlane GetAlpha](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_getalpha.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_handle GetAlpha` | This command returns the alpha or transparency setting for the image plane. The value returned is from 0 – 100, where 100 is completely opaque, and 0 is completely transparent. The transparency of an image plane in 3D mode is also affected by the Cull Angle settings. | Not stated |
| [poIImagePlane GetBackFaceVisibility](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_getbackfacevisibility.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_handle GetBackFaceVisibility` | When an image plane is in 3D mode, the model and image plane may get rotated so that the camera is looking at the back of the plane. The back face visibility setting determines whether the image plane remains visible when the back face is exposed. This command returns the value of the back face visibility setting. | Not stated |
| [poIImagePlane GetColorAlphaTarget](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_getcoloralphatarget.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_handle GetColorAlphaTarget` | The image plane offers several image post-processing filters that can be applied on the image or video. The ColorAlpha filter makes the target color in the image or video transparent. This command returns the RGB value of the target color as a series of three values between 0 and 255. | Not stated |
| [poIImagePlane GetColorAlphaTolerance](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_getcoloralphatolerance.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_handle GetColorAlphaTolerance` | The image plane offers several image post-processing filters that can be applied on the image or video. The ColorAlpha filter makes the target color in the image or video transparent. This command returns the search tolerance for the target color. This only applies when the filter mode is set to “equal”. The returned value is an integer representing a decimal percentage that is applied to the color target when searching. | Not stated |
| [poIImagePlane GetCullAngle](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_getcullangle.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_handle GetCullAngle` | When the image plane mode is 3D, HyperView compares the camera view angle to the image plane’s surface normal. When the difference is greater than the cull angle, HyperView applies transparency to the image plane. The greater the angle difference, the more transparent it becomes. The transparency effect is also multiplied by the Alpha property of the image plane. This command returns the current cull angle value. | Not stated |
| [poIImagePlane GetEdgeColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_getedgecolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_handle GetEdgeColor` | The image plane offers several image post-processing filters that can be applied on the image or video. One of them is an edge detection filter, where detected edges in the image are rendered with a specified color. This command returns the RGB values for the color of the rendered edges. Returns the RGB edge color, as three values between 0 and 255. | Not stated |
| [poIImagePlane GetFileName](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_getfilename.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_handle GetFileName` | Each image plane is associated with a image or video file. This command returns the file name of the image or video. | Not stated |
| [poIImagePlane GetFilterMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_getfiltermode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_handle GetFilterMode` | The image plane offers several image post-processing filters that can be applied on the image or video. This command returns the index of the last frame with data. Starting from the beginning, it gives the number of frames that have tracking data. After this many frames, the video will not be tracked. | Not stated |
| [poIImagePlane GetMipMapMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_getmipmapmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_handle GetMipMapMode` | Mipmaps are a way to shrink an image while retaining detail, before rending the final image to the display. Some mipmap modes work better than others, depending on the type of image being drawn and how much it is being shrunk. This command returns the current mipmap mode for an image plane. | Not stated |
| [poIImagePlane GetMipMapModeList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_getmipmapmodelist.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_handle GetMipMapModeList` | Mipmaps are a way to shrink an image while retaining detail, before rending the final image to the display. Some mipmap modes work better than others, depending on the type of image being drawn and how much it is being shrunk. This command returns the list of mipmap modes that can be specified for an image plane. | Not stated |
| [poIImagePlane GetMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_getmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_handle GetMode` | Image planes support three drawing modes: foreground, background, and 3D. In the foreground and background modes, the image or video is drawn as a flat rectangle, either in front of all the 3D model elements in the graphic area, or behind all the 3D model elements in the graphic area, respectively. In 3D mode, the image plane is drawn as a plane in three dimensional model space. It follows the rotation, zoom, and panning of the rest of the model, and can be also be aligned so that the camera angle of the image or video matches the 3D position of the plane relative to the FE model. This command returns which mode the image plane is in. | Not stated |
| [poIImagePlane GetNumAlignmentPoints](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_getnumalignmentpoints.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_handle GetNumAlignmentPoints` | This command returns the number of alignment points in an image plane. | Not stated |

| [poIImagePlane GetSyncBlockID](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_getsyncblockid.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_handle GetSyncBlockID` | Unique animation sync block IDs are used by HyperWorks to identify each source of animated frames. Length The video and model start and stop animating at the same time. Framerate The video and model animate at the same number of frames per second. | Not stated |
| [poIImagePlane GetSyncModel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_getsyncmodel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_handle GetSyncModel` | This command returns the model being synchronized with the image plane. | Not stated |
| [poIImagePlane GetSyncModeList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_getsyncmodelist.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_handle GetSyncModeList` | This command returns a list of valid sync modes: Manual, Length, or Framerate. | Not stated |

Model The associated model ID determines which model is used to follow an active tracking system. VideoMarker The calculated marker tracking information is needed. The tracking mode allows the image plane to follow a tracking system on a Model, or to follow the position of a marker in a video as it moves. | Not stated |
| [poIImagePlane GetTrackingModeList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_gettrackingmodelist.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_handle GetTrackingModeList` | This command returns the list of tracking modes available for an image plane: None, Model, and VideoMarker. | Not stated |
| [poIImagePlane GetUserTransformList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_getusertrandformlist.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_handle GetUserTransformList` | The image plane maintains a separate user transform to track the position, rotation, and scaling applied to the image plane. This command returns the current transform. The returned data is different, depending on whether the image plane is in 3D mode or in foreground/background mode. In 3D mode, the transform string consists of twelve floats, representing the rotation about X, Y, and Z, and the translation in X, Y, and Z. For foreground/background modes, the transform string consists of four floats, representing the X and Y scaling factor followed by the X and Y translation for the image. This command does not return any data regarding alignment of a 3D image plane to a model. See GetAlignmentData for this information. | Not stated |
| [poIImagePlane GetVidTrackData](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_getvidtrackdata.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_handle GetVidTrackData` | This command returns the video tracking data used to track a point. This is primarily used for creating session files. The string returned is in the form “x y X Y X Y X Y…..” , where x and y are floats for the pixel location of the tracking point and the remaining X's and Y's are the offsets per frame order from beginning to end. | Not stated |

| [poIImagePlaneCtrl GetZOrderList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplanectrl_getzorderlist.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `Imageplane_ctrl_handle GetZOrderList type` | Image planes have one of three modes for placement into the client window: foreground, background, or 3D. Background image planes are always drawn behind the model elements. Foreground image planes are always drawn in front of the model elements. When multiple background or foreground image planes are present, the Z order controls how overlapping image planes are drawn with respect to one another. This command returns the order in which image planes are drawn for the specified type. | [poIPost GetResultList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_getresultlist.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `post_handle GetResultList context` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, returns a list of defined result IDs of the given type. | [poIResultMathCtrl GetAlias](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultmathctrl_getalias.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `rmath_handle GetAlias delim str` | This command retrieves the alias string corresponding to the input string. This string is used by the result math expression builder to display a table specification in short hand form. In order for this command to work, the input string should contain the following six values: Resource name Subcase ID Simulation index Datatype name Datacomponent name Layer name Note: If you do not want to specify a particular field for a table specifier, that field must be left blank. See the usage of this command for the bumper d3plot model in the example section below. | **Inputs:** delim The delimiter string used to separate the different fields of a table specification. str The input string for the desired alias. |

| [poIResultMathCtrl GetOperatorSyntax](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultmathctrl_getoperatorsyntax.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `rmath_handle GetOperatorSyntax lib oper` | This command returns the syntax of a result math operator. | **Inputs:** lib The name of the result math library. oper The name of the result math operator. |
| [poIResultMathCtrl GetResourceFilePath](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultmathctrl_getresourcefilepath.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `rmath_handle GetResourceFilePath external_resource_label` | This command returns the path of the file which was used to add an external result math resource. When a model and result file is loaded with the “Standard” result math template, two default resources will be created (“model” and “result”). Please note that if the same file is used as model and result, then only one resource called “model” will be created. In HyperView, you can add additional resource files using the AddResource command. Every resource that is added will have a label. This command does not return the file path of default resources “model” and “result” (you can use GetFileName and GetResultFileName on the model handle for getting the file paths of default resources), it only returns the file path for resources added using the AddResource command. | **Inputs:** external_resource_label The label of the external resource. |
| [poIResultMathCtrl GetResourceLabel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultmathctrl_getresourcelabel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `rmath_handle GetResourceLabel sub_id` | This command returns the file name corresponding to the subcase ID. This command is commonly used when multiple result files are loaded using a result math template. | **Inputs:** sub_id The subcase ID. |
| [poIResultMathCtrl GetResourceList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultmathctrl_getresourcelist.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `rmath_handle GetResourceList type` | This command retrieves a list of result module resources. The argument “type” is optional. If the argument has a value of “external”, only the external resources (added by the command AddResource) will be listed. If the argument is omitted, all of the resources will be listed. When a model/result file is loaded into HyperView, there may one or two resources depending on whether the model and result files are the same. For example, if the same d3plot is used for model and result, there will be only one resource. | **Inputs:** type Valid options are: "external" or "all". |

The first item contained in the list is the format of the table (scalar/vector/tensor), and the second item in the list is the binding of the table (node/element/part). | **Inputs:** table_name The name of the table whose information is needed. res_name (Optional) The resource name of the table. |

Return values can either be a list of IDs of all entities matching the specified pool name or if no pool name was specified, a list of all entity IDs included in the selection set. This list may be very large. | **Inputs:** pool_name (Optional) If not set, all entity IDs are listed, otherwise only the IDs for the entities matching the specified pool name are listed. |

| [poISelectionSet GetRangeList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiselectionset_getrangelist.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `selectionSet_handle GetRangeList pool_name` | This command returns a list of pool name and ID ranges of either all entities or entities matching the optional pool_name argument. | **Inputs:** pool_name (Optional) If set, the command returns a range list of entities matching the specific pool name. | [poISelectionSet GetSize](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiselectionset_getsize.htm) | HyperView Tcl Query | `selectionSet_handle GetSize` | This command returns the number of entities of the selection set. The circle center is found by using the three nodes which lie on the circumference of the circle. | **Inputs:** node1_loc The first node on the circle circumference. node2_loc The second node on the circle circumference. node3_loc The third node on the circle circumference. [movetype] The move type of the system: true - Fixed (Default), false - Moving. |
